def lse_score_grad(a, beta):
    n=len(a); c=fast_conv(a); S=np.sum(a)
    if S <= 0: return 1e10, zeros
    c_max=np.max(c)
    scaled = beta*(c-c_max)
    exp_scaled = np.exp(scaled)
    # if sum under/overflow? scaled <=0, okay if beta finite; exp sum ≤len. For beta large, scaled can be very negative; fine. Use logsumexp with stable: max scaled 0.
    lse = np.log(exp_scaled.sum())
    L = c_max + lse/beta
    score = 2*n*L/(S*S)
    p = exp_scaled/exp_scaled.sum()
    # gradient: need h_j = sum_k p_k * 2 * a_{k-j} (with boundaries)
    # This is 2 * (p conv rev? previous: conv_res = irfft(RA*P)[n-1:2n-1]

    # resample a0 to n_target if needed
    a = resample(a0, n_target)
    # normalize sum to 1

Implement softmax stable:
```
def softmax_from_theta(theta):
    m = theta.max()
    ex = np.exp(theta - m)
    a = ex / ex.sum()
    if a.sum() <=0: lower...
    return a
```
If θ has very varied values, ones with θ-m< -745 become 0. Good.

Implement `resample_sequence(a0, n)`:
```
if len(a0)==n: return a0
x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(a0)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a0)
new=np.clip(new,0,None)
s=new.sum()
if s>0: new/=s
else: new=np.ones(n)/n
return new
```

```
def continuation_optimize(a, beta_schedule, time_budget):
    best_a=a; best_sc=compute_score(a)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    for beta in beta_schedule:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_budget: break
       theta = np.log(a + 1e-12) - np.log(a).max()? 
       # choose maxiter based on n and remaining: iter cost ~? use 800 lower n, 300 higher? 
       res = minimize(softmax_lse_objective, theta, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', maxiter=iter, maxfun=maxiter*5)
       a_new=softmax(res.x)
       sc=compute_score(a_new)
       if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new.copy()
       a = a_new.copy()
       # line search exact? maybe
    # exact polish best_a
    return best_sc,best_a
```

Let's implement `fast_conv` efficient:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n=len(a)
    M=2*n-1
    Mf=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A, Mf)[:M]
    # numerical tiny negative? clip?
    return b
```
For gradient need conv of p and rev_a. p length 2n-1, rev length n, output length 3n-2, slice. Use M2_fft = 1 << (3*n - 2 -1).bit_length(). Could be large; okay.
```
def convolved_p_with_rev(p, a):
    n=len(a); p_len=2*n-1
    M= n + p_len -1
    Mf=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, Mf)
    RA=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Mf)
    out=np.fft.irfft(P*RA, Mf)
    return out[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check slice length n. For p length 3, rev n maybe. We'll trust previous.

Could avoid FFT for gradient by direct top active? Since p sparse, gradient = sum p_k * shift. For top K small, direct O(Kn) faster. For LSE with high beta p sparse, use direct if active count small. ```

Let's detail each.

`fast_conv` maybe small arrays: if n<256 use np.convolve for simplicity? FFT overhead. But n large. Use FFT.

`conv_p_rev(p,a)`:
```
def conv_p_rev(p,a):
    n=len(a)
    M=n+len(p)-1
    Mf=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, Mf)
    RA=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Mf)
    out=np.fft.irfft(P*RA, Mf)
    return out[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check length: `out[n-1:2*n-1]` length n. For p length lenp, output start index n-1? previous. Good.

`lse_lag_grad`:
```
def lse_lag_grad(a,beta):
    n=len(a)
    c=fast_conv(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    # scaled <=0, but due FP maybe small positive? use np.clip(scaled, None, 0)
    exps=np.exp(scaled)
    total=float(exps.sum())
    if total<=0: p=np.zeros_like(c); M=cmax
    else: p=exps/total; M=cmax + np.log(total)/beta
    gradM=2.0*conv_p_rev(p,a)
    return M, gradM
```
Potential overflow with beta large and c-cmax negative: exp underflows to0, fine. If cmax huge? a sum1, c ~1/n. beta 1e5 okay. If a direct unscaled sum maybe varied; scale invariant but M may be large; scaled beta*(c-cmax) could overflow positive if cmax not max due FP? Use scaled = beta*(c - cmax); scaled=np.minimum(scaled,0); exp safer. If beta*1e-309? fine.

`direct_score_grad`:

score=2*n*M/(S*S)
grad=2*n*(gradM/(S*S) - 2*M/(S*S*S))
return score, grad
```
Check: grad term constant for all j? Yes but should be vector `- 2*M/S^3` multiplied by ones. `grad` array = gradM/S2 - (2M/S3)*np.ones(n). But previous used same. To ensure sum a * grad =0: sum a gradM/S2 -2M S/S3 = (2M*S? wait ∑a gradM =2M? M degree2? For M(a) = LSE approximating max c, homogeneous degree2? If scaled washing, ∑a gradM = 2M exactly for hard max, approx for LSE? LSE is not exactly homogeneous? It is scale equivariant up to additive? LSE(λc,β)=λ LSE(c,β) only if β scaled? But scale invariance of f? f(a,beta) is not scale invariant for fixed beta? c scaled, beta fixed LSE not exactly scale equivariant. But as β→∞ yes. Good.

`softmax_lse_grad`:
```
def softmax_from_theta(theta):
    m=float(np.max(theta))
    ex=np.exp(theta - m)
    a=ex / ex.sum()
    if not finite or sum0: a=zeros? return ones/n
    return a

def softmax_lse_grad(theta,beta):
    n=theta.size
    a=softmax_from_theta(theta)
    M,gradM=lse_lag_grad(a,beta)
    score=2.0*n*M  # sum=1
    # projection
    c=np.dot(a, gradM)
    grad_theta=a*(gradM - c*a)
    # remove numerical mean? maybe
    return score, grad_theta
```
Because objective independent of scale; sum fixed. If a has zeros from underflow, grad_theta for zero entries zero? a=0, okay.

Now, exact active polish implementation:
```
def active_polish(a, n_iter=200, top_k=64, beta_mode=20, step_base=0.1):
    n=len(a); best=a.copy(); bestsc=compute_score(a)
    it=0
    while it<n_iter and time...:
        c=fast_conv(a); M=float(c.max()); S=float(a.sum())
        # top lags
        K=min(top_k, 2*n-1)
        # select indices of top K c values. Use argpartition for speed.
        if K >= len(c): idx=np.arange(len(c)); vals=c
        else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]; vals=c[idx]
        # weights softmax beta_mode over vals
        v=vals - vals.max(); w=np.exp(beta_mode*v); w/=w.sum(); p=zeros; p[idx]=w
        q=2*conv_p_rev(p,a)
        # direction under sum constraint: d = q - (q.mean())? But to improve score, use gradient of score: grad = 2n(q/S2 - 2M/S3)
        grad_score = 2*n*(q/(S*S) - 2*M/(S*S*S))  # constant term vector? This is q/S2 - const
        # project to sum-zero: d = -grad_score - mean? Since gradient varies by q_j - const; mean = mean(q)/S2. Subtract mean(grad) = mean(q)/S2 - const. Projection onto sum zero: d = -(grad_score - mean(grad_score))
        d = -(q - q.mean())  # since constant cancels. This reduces high q.
        # scale step: alpha = step_base * (np.linalg.norm(a)/(np.linalg.norm(d)+eps))
        # Multiplicative update: a_try = a * exp(-alpha * d / scale)
        scale = np.percentile(np.abs(d[a>0]), 50) + eps
        for trial in range(6):
           a_try = a * np.exp(np.clip(-alpha * d, -5, 5)?) Wait alpha*d/scale already clipped.
           a_try = np.clip(a_try,0,None)
           s=a_try.sum(); if s>0: a_try/=s
           sc=compute_score(a_try)
           ...
           if improved: a=a_try; break else alpha*=0.5
        it++
    return best_sc,best_a
```
But `q - q.mean()` may have large values at endpoints. Multiplicative with d could drive zeros. Need stable.
Alternatively direct add: `a_try = np.clip(a - alpha * d,0,None)/sum`. Since a entries ~1/n, d scale ~? q_j maybe ~M? Clamp. Use line search.

Let's implement a `subgradient_topk_polish` simpler:
```
def topk_polish(a, n_iter=200, top_k=32, beta_smooth=10, min_step=1e-6):
    best=...; a=a/s
    for it in range(n_iter):
       c=fast_conv(a); M=c.max(); K=top_k
       idx=argpartition(c,-K)[-K:]
       p=zeros; p[idx]=1/K
       q=2*conv_p_rev(p,a)
       # steepest descent for M with sum fixed: d = q - mean(q) (unweighted). But maybe use weighted by a? Softmore: grad_theta? For a direct, use d = q - (np.sum(a*q)/np.sum(a))? To keep ∑a? Hmm.
       d = q - np.mean(q)
       # step size alpha = 0.1 / max(1, ||d||/||a||)
       alpha=0.5 * np.linalg.norm(a)/ (np.linalg.norm(d)+1e-12)
       # try a - alpha*d; clip, renorm; line search
       best_sc_iter=...
       for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.01]:
          a_try=np.clip(a - alpha*factor*d,0,None)
          s=a_try.sum(); if s<=0: continue; a_try/=s
          sc=compute_score(a_try)
          if sc < best_sc: best=a_try; best_sc=sc; a=a_try; break
          # also score < current?
       if no improvement: maybe smaller top_k? if all iterations no improve break
    return best_sc,best_a
```
Need ensure d correct. The gradient of M (not score) is q=2∑p a_{k-j}. If we minimize M under sum fixed, projected gradient direction d = q - mean(q) (since constraint ∑δ=0). Descend a - α d. If q has high values where gradient high (coeffs contributing to active peaks), reduce them. Good.

But q magnitude: a~1/n, c~1/n, q ~? For uniform, p maybe all lags, q ~? d small. Step scaling by norm works.

Could miss improvements if all active q constant (KKT). Then polish stops.

def skew_cubic_sequence(n, lam):
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if n==1: return np.array([1.0])
    # to avoid exact zero endpoints? use x clip [1e-6,1-1e-6]

    f = np.clip(f, 0, None)

    return f/s

`lowpass` function:
```
def lowpass_sequence(a, sigma=1.0):
    n=len(a)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a)
    k=np.fft.rfftfreq(n, d=1.0/n)  # cycles per unit? p=0..n/2
    filt=np.exp(-(sigma*2*np.pi*k)**2)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*filt, n)
    out=np.clip(out,0,None)
    s=out.sum(); if s>0 out/=s
    return out
```

Let's refine `topk_polish` math. We want reduce score R=2n max c / S^2. Since scale invariant, normalize S=1. Minimize M=max c. Use top active p. Gradient of smoothed max q=2 conv_p_rev(p,a). To maintain sum=1, we can do a line search in direction d = -q + mean(q)/? under L2 metric. But with positivity, multiplicative update using gradient of softmax objective may be better:
For softmax variables θ, update θ -= α ∇_θ M = α a(q - q·a a). Then a_new=softmax(θ). This is equivalent to a_new_j ∝ a_j exp(-α(q_j - ∑ a q a_j)). Since ∑a q =? For p weights, ∑a q =2∑p c_k = 2M_p (where M_p weighted active avg). This multiplicative update preserves positivity and sum. We can implement this as polish without L-BFGS:
```
θ = log(a+eps)
for it:
  c=fast_conv(a); select p top K; M_p=∑p c
  q=2conv_p_rev(p,a)
  gθ = a*(q - dot(a,q)*a)
  # choose step α based on norm; line search score
  for trial α in [0.1,0.05,...]:
      θ_try = θ - α * scale * gθ
      a_try=softmax(θ_try)
      sc=compute_score(a_try)
      improve? 
  θ=θ_try
```
This can directly improve M while preserving sum. It may be more stable than direct add. Let's implement `softmax_topk_polish(a, n_iter, top_k, alphas)`.
- Start θ = log(a + eps) - mean.
- Compute p as softmax with beta_p over top K or all? We can use top K uniform. For smoother, p = softmax(beta_policy*(c - cmax)) over all; this is exactly LSE with hard active? We can use top K uniform.
- Direction gθ as above.

def softmax_topk_polish(a, n_iter=200, top_k=64, beta_p=0, time_limit=np.inf, t_start=0):
    n=len(a); a=a/s; theta=np.log(a+1e-15); theta-=theta.mean()
    best=a.copy(); bestsc=compute_score(a); theta_best=theta.copy()
    alphas=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]
    for it in range(n_iter):
       if time.monotonic() > time_limit: break
       c=fast_conv(a)
       if beta_p > 0:
          cm=c.max(); vals=exp(beta_p*(c-cm)); total; p=vals/total; # maybe threshold p>0
       else:
          K=min(top_k,len(c)); idx=np.argpartition(c,-K)[-K:]; p=zeros; p[idx]=1/K
       q=2*conv_p_rev(p,a)
       g=a*(q - np.dot(a,q)*a)
       maxg=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if maxg < 1e-12: break
       improved=False
       for alpha in alphas:
           theta_try = theta - alpha * g / maxg
           # keep max magnitude not too large: if theta_try.max()-theta.max()>? but okay
           a_try=softmax_from_theta(theta_try)
           sc=compute_score(a_try)

               theta=theta_try; a=a_try; improved=True; break

           # try smaller
           theta_try = theta - 0.001 * g/maxg
           a_try=softmax(theta_try); sc=compute_score(a_try)
           if sc<bestsc: ... else break? 
    return bestsc,best
```

Let's write `make_base_from_parent`:
```
def sanitize_sequence(seq):
    arr=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr=arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    if arr.size==0: arr=np.ones(1024)*0.5
    s=arr.sum()
    if s>0: arr=arr/s
    else: arr=np.ones(arr.size)/arr.size if arr.size>0 else np.ones(1024)/1024
    return arr
```
Parent sum normalized; score invariant.

`beta_sequence`:
```
def beta_sequence(n, alpha,beta):
    if n ==1: return np.array([1.0])
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    eps=1e-12
    x1=x+eps; x2=1-x+eps? Need avoid 0**negative? alpha,beta nonnegative, 0**0=1 if use np.power? 0**0 in numpy returns1? Yes. But for negative not. Our params >=0. Safe.
    f=np.power(x, alpha) * np.power(1-x, beta)
    f=np.clip(f,0,None)
    s=f.sum()
    if s<=0: f=np.ones(n)
    else: f=f/s
    return f
```
If alpha=0, x^0 =1 including 0; endpoint positive. Good.

Maybe endpoint coefficient a0^2 can be high; beta with α=0 gives endpoint ~1/n? square ~1/n^2, max coefficient elsewhere ~1/n; endpoint negligible. Fine.

Another initial: **Pointwise square root of target flat sequence?** Suppose we want P^2 coefficients target t_k = C for k=0..2n-2 sum to 1 -> C=1/(2n-1). Need P nonnegative with P^2 approx t. If t is constant polynomial, P(x)=sqrt(C ∑_{k=0}^{2n-2}x^k) not polynomial. But for trigonometric on circle, choose F with flat squared modulus. On finite interval, perhaps use **Rudin-Shapiro** Walsh? Could construct via iterative inverse of FFT: Start with desired periodogram H flat, take square root with random phases, inverse FFT, take real positive part. This is like nonnegative inverse problems. For polynomial square coefficients, if c_k is target, its DFT C(ω)=∑c_k e^{-iωk}=P(e^{iω})^2 (for k 0..2n-2, one-sided). P coefficients are first half of inverse DFT of sqrt(C) with appropriate phases. We can choose phases to make P real nonnegative. This is spectral factorization of a nonnegative trigonometric polynomial. If c_k has zero wrap-around (length 2n-1 padded odd), C(ω) should be nonnegative. We can design c_k nonnegative with sum1 and flat, pad to odd length, ensure its DTFT nonnegative? Not guaranteed. But we can start with c target and iteratively project:
- Given target c (length 2n-1), compute its DFT using real nonnegative? Need factor √C with phase such that inverse real is first n coefficients of P and last n-1 mirror? For P^2, if P real length n, C(ω)=(∑_{j=0}^{n-1}p_j e^{-ijω})^2. The square root phase must be a minimum-phase filter? We can use spectral factorization: If C(ω) is nonnegative even? Since P not conjugate? P(e^{iω})^2 generally complex, not even. C(ω) not necessarily real. So factoring one-sided polynomial square is not simple.

But we can use **iterative hard thresholding** on P: For a chosen amplitude spectrum |P(ω)| corresponding to flat P^2, reconstruct P with phases and enforce nonnegative/nonnegative support. Too complex.

def normalize_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12:
       a=np.ones(a.size,dtype=np.float64)
       s=a.size
    else:
       a=a/s
    return a
```

       np?

`lowpass_sequence`:
```
def lowpass_sequence(a,sigma=0.8):
    n=len(a)
    if n<4: return normalize_array(a)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a)
    k=np.fft.rfftfreq(n, d=1.0/n)  # cycles per unit length? If x grid 0..1 with spacing1/n, frequencies cycles per unit.
    filt=np.exp(-(sigma*k)**2)  # if sigma in unit cycles? Use 2π? For discrete, k up 0.5, sigma 1 decays.
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*filt,n)
    out=np.clip(out,0,None)
    return normalize_array(out)
```

```
def compute_score(a):
    n=len(a); S=float(np.sum(a));
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=fast_conv(a)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if M<0: M=0
    return 2*n*M/(S*S)
```

Detailed `softmax_topk_polish`:
```
def softmax_topk_polish(a, n_iter=100, top_k=64, beta_p=0, time_limit=np.inf):
    a=normalize_array(a)
    n=len(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
    cur=a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
    theta=np.log(a+1e-15); theta-=theta.mean()
    alphas=[0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(n_iter):
       if time.monotonic() > time_limit: break
       c=fast_conv(cur)
       if beta_p > 0:
           cm=float(np.max(c))
           scaled=beta_p*(c-cm); scaled=np.minimum(scaled,0)
           ep=np.exp(scaled); total=ep.sum()
           if total <=0: p=None
           else: p=ep/total
           # maybe zero tiny: p[p<1e-8]=0; renorm
           # But conv_p_rev p all lags. If p small, gradient ok.
       else:
           K=min(top_k, len(c))
           if K <=0: break
           idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
           p=np.zeros(len(c)); p[idx]=1.0/K
       q=2.0*conv_p_rev(p,cur)
       g=cur*(q - float(np.dot(cur,q))*cur)
       mg=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if mg < 1e-13: break
       improved=False
       # try alphas descending? Start small for stability? Try large to small.
       for alpha in alphas:
           theta_try = theta - alpha * g / mg
           a_try=softmax_from_theta(theta_try)
           sc=compute_score(a_try)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
               best_sc=sc; best=a_try.copy(); theta_best=theta_try.copy()
               # accept for current if sc < cur_sc or best improved?
               if sc < cur_sc:
                   cur=a_try.copy(); cur_sc=sc; theta=theta_try.copy()
               improved=True
               break
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc * (1.0 + 1e-12) and not improved:
               # accept if not worse significantly? Maybe not
               pass
       if not improved:
           # try a very small step
           theta_try = theta - 1e-4 * g / mg
           a_try=softmax_from_theta(theta_try)
           sc=compute_score(a_try)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc:
               cur=a_try.copy(); cur_sc=sc; theta=theta_try.copy(); improved=True
           else:
               no_improve += 1
               if no_improve > 5: break
       else:
           no_improve=0
    return best_sc,best
```

def mixture_from_params_k3(params, n):
    lw = params[0:3]; la=params[3:6]; lb=params[6:9]
    w = np.exp(-np.abs(lw))? No, logw can be any but positive. Use softmax on lw for weights? Better: weights = softmax(params[0:3]), shapes=exp(params[3:6]) clipped, beta=exp(params[6:9]) clipped. This avoids logw scaling. Parameters unbounded. 
    al = np.clip(np.exp(la), 1e-3, 8.0)
    be = np.clip(np.exp(lb), 1e-3, 8.0)
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    f=np.zeros(n)
    for i in range(3):
        f += w_i * np.power(x, al[i]) * np.power(1-x, be[i])
    f=np.clip(f,0,None); s=f.sum(); if s>0 f/=s else uniform
    return f
def mixture_score_k3(params, n):
    _check_timeout()
    f=mixture_from_params_k3(params,n)
    return float(compute_score(f))
```

Mixture function with fixed K=3 top-level:
```
def shape_params_to_density_k3(params, n):
    # params length 9
    w = np.zeros(3); w[:] = np.exp(params[0:3] - max)
    w /= sum
    al = np.exp(params[3:6]); be = np.exp(params[6:9])
    al=np.clip(al,1e-3,8.0); be=np.clip(be,1e-3,8.0)
    if n<=1: return np.array([1.0])
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    f=np.zeros(n)
    # vectorized over components? K small; loop.
    for i in range(3):
       f += w[i]*np.power(x, al[i])*np.power(1-x, be[i])
    return normalize_array(f)
```
`np.power` with x including 1 and be>0 -> 0; al>0. For al clipped >0, endpoints zero. If we want allow endpoint positive, al could be 0. Clipped 1e-3 makes endpoint ~0, maybe too restrictive. Beta densities with α=0 could be useful (finite endpoint). But target endpoints maybe low. Allow α,β in [0,8] but handle 0. Use if al_i <=0: factor=1 else x**al. Since exp(log) >0 never 0; can clip lower 0.0? If parameter logα very negative, we can set al=0 if <1e-6. Let's clip to [0.0,8.0]. If al=0, `x**0=1`. Good.

`M, gradM = lse_lag_grad(a,beta)`. For sum fixed a, `score=2n*M`. `c=np.dot(a,gradM)`. `grad_theta=a*(gradM - c*a)`. This assumes objective M (not score) and sum fixed. This gradient is for optimizing M over probability simplex, not necessarily maximizing sum? Yes. So `grad_theta` correct for fixed sum. Good.

def make_lengths(parent_len, max_n=24576):
    vals=[parent_len, int(parent_len*1.5), int(parent_len*2), 16384, max_n]
    out=[]
    for v in vals:
       if v is None: continue
       v=int(round(v))
       if v < 256: v=256
       if v > max_n: v=max_n
       if v not in out:
          out.append(v)
    out.sort()
    if not out: out=[8192]
    # If parent_len already > max_n, remove small? Keep parent.
    # Limit to 3:
    if len(out)>3:
       # keep smallest, middle, largest? But parent might be not smallest. Keep unique sorted and if >3 keep [out[0], out[len(out)//2], out[-1]]
       out=[out[0], out[len(out)//2], out[-1]]
    return out
```

```
def optimize_sequence(a0, time_budget, target_n):
    if time_budget <=0: a0=normalize; return compute_score(a0),a0
    a0=resample_sequence(a0,target_n)
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    a=normalize_array(a0)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
    if not finite: best_sc=1e12
    init_sc=best_sc
    if init_sc > 1.8: betas=[10,30,100,300,1000,3000,10000]
    else: betas=[30,100,300,1000,3000,10000]
    est_iter=estimate_objective_iter_time(target_n)
    for i,beta in enumerate(betas):
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-t_start
       if elapsed > time_budget - 30: break
       n_left=len(betas)-i
       call_seconds=max(10.0, (time_budget - elapsed - 20.0)*0.45 / max(1, n_left))
       maxiter=int(max(20, min(3000, call_seconds / max(est_iter,1e-4))))
       theta=np.log(a+1e-15); theta-=theta.max()
       res=run_lbfgs_softmax(theta,beta,maxiter)
       if res is None: break
       a_new=softmax_from_theta(res.x)
       sc_new=compute_score(a_new)
       if finite and sc_new < best_sc: update
       if finite: a=a_new
       # Occasionally polish if beta large
       if beta >= 300:
           pol_budget=min(90.0, max(5.0, 0.2*(time_budget-(time.monotonic()-t_start))))
           if pol_budget > 10:
              scp,ap=softmax_topk_polish(a, n_iter=80, top_k=64, beta_p=0, max_seconds=pol_budget)
              if finite and scp < best_sc: update
              if finite: a=ap.copy()
    # final polish best
    elapsed=time.monotonic()-t_start
    rem=time_budget-elapsed
    if rem > 20:
       scp,bp=softmax_topk_polish(best, n_iter=300, top_k=128, beta_p=0, max_seconds=min(120.0, rem*0.6))
       update
       if rem > 50:
       scp2,bp2=softmax_topk_polish(best, n_iter=150, top_k=512, beta_p=20, max_seconds=min(60.0, rem*0.3))
       update
    return best_sc,best
```

       if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 60: break
       tb=alloc...; tb=min(tb, hard_deadline-time.monotonic()-30)
       if tb <= 5: break
       # choose initial for n
       init_a=None; init_sc=inf
       for base in base_seqs:
           if base is None: continue
           a0=resample_sequence(base,n)
           sc=compute_score(a0)
           if finite and sc<init_sc: init...
       # Beta scan large if n<=16384 and (init_sc > 1.6 or idx==0)
       if n <= 16384:
           a_b, sc_b, _,_=scan_beta(n, quick=True)
           if finite and sc_b<init_sc: init=sc_b, a_b
       # Also if base best_a length n, ensure considered.
       if init_a is None: init_a=resample_sequence(best_a,n); init_sc=compute_score(init_a)
       sc_opt,a_opt=optimize_sequence(init_a, tb, n)
       if finite and sc_opt<best_score: best_score=sc_opt; best_a=a_opt.copy()
       base_seqs.append(a_opt)
```
This will optimize parent length, mid, max in ascending order. `optimize_sequence` uses `base_seqs` improved from previous. Good.

Final polish: remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 20. If >30, call `softmax_topk_polish(best_a, n_iter=500, top_k=128, beta_p=0, max_seconds=min(120, remaining*0.5))`. If improved update. Also maybe try zeroing small:

```python
"""
Multi-scale optimizer for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))**2
over non-negative sequences.

The optimizer combines:
  * a low-dimensional mixture-of-betas model in the frequency/shape space,
  * softmax-parameterized L-BFGS on an LSE-smoothed maximum-convolution objective,
  * top-lag multiplicative (softmax-space) polishing,
  * hierarchical length scaling of the best shapes.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


class OptimizationTimeout(Exception):
    pass


_TIME_LIMIT = np.inf


def _check_timeout():
    if time.monotonic() > _TIME_LIMIT:
        raise OptimizationTimeout()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic numerical utilities
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    a = a / s
    return a


def sanitize_sequence(seq):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(np.sum(a)) <= 0.0:
        return None
    a = a / float(np.sum(a))
    return a


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return normalize_array(np.ones(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64))
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(a.size)
    if m == n:
        out = a.copy()
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_array(out)


def lowpass_sequence(a, sigma=0.35):
    a = normalize_array(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 16:
        return a
    A = np.fft.rfft(a)
    k = np.fft.rfftfreq(n, d=1.0 / n)  # cycles per unit interval [0,1]
    filt = np.exp(-(2.0 * np.pi * sigma * k) ** 2)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * filt, n)
    return normalize_array(out)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Convolution and smooth objectives
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.asarray([float(a[0] * a[0]) if n == 1 else 0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    Mf = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, Mf)[:M]
    return out


def conv_p_rev(p, a):
    """
    Returns g[j] = sum_k p[k] * a[k-j] over valid k-j.
    This is used for gradient of max(a*a) with respect to a.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if p.size == 0 or a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    M = n + p.size - 1
    Mf = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, Mf)
    RA = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Mf)
    out = np.fft.irfft(P * RA, Mf)
    return out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def lse_lag_grad(a, beta):
    """
    LSE-smoothed max of a*a and its gradient w.r.t. a.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = float(max(1.0, beta))
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    scaled = np.minimum(scaled, 0.0)
    ex = np.exp(scaled)
    total = float(np.sum(ex))
    if total <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(total):
        # Fallback to uniform weights over top coefficients.
        k = min(64, c.size)
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = 1.0 / k
        M = cmax
    else:
        p = ex / total
        M = cmax + float(np.log(total)) / beta
    gradM = 2.0 * conv_p_rev(p, a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gradM)):
        gradM = np.where(np.isfinite(gradM), gradM, 0.0)
    return float(M), gradM


def compute_score(a):
    a = normalize_array(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.inf
    if M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Softmax-parameterized objective (sum fixed to 1)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def softmax_from_theta(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    n = theta.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    m = float(np.max(theta))
    ex = np.exp(theta - m)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    a = ex / s
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    ss = float(np.sum(a))
    if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return a / ss


def softmax_lse_grad(theta, beta):
    _check_timeout()
    n = theta.size
    a = softmax_from_theta(theta)
    M, gradM = lse_lag_grad(a, beta)
    score = 2.0 * n * M
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = float(np.dot(a, gradM))
    grad_theta = a * (gradM - c * a)
    grad_theta = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_theta), grad_theta, 0.0)
    return float(score), grad_theta


def softmax_objective(theta, beta):
    return softmax_lse_grad(theta, beta)


def run_lbfgs_softmax(theta0, beta, maxiter):
    try:
        maxiter = int(max(20, maxiter))
        res = minimize(
            softmax_objective,
            np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64),
            args=(float(beta),),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": max(1000, 10 * maxiter),
                "ftol": 1e-16,
                "gtol": 1e-11,
                "maxls": 50,
                "maxcorr": 20,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        return res
    except OptimizationTimeout:
        return None
    except Exception:
        return None


def estimate_objective_iter_time(n):
    try:
        n = max(64, int(n))
        theta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        softmax_lse_grad(theta, 50.0)
        t1 = time.monotonic()
        return max(1e-4, 8.0 * (t1 - t0))
    except Exception:
        return max(0.01, 1e-5 * n)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exact / top-lag multiplicative polish in softmax space
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def softmax_topk_polish(a, n_iter=150, top_k=64, beta_p=0.0, max_seconds=np.inf):
    global _TIME_LIMIT
    a = normalize_array(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, a
    old_limit = _TIME_LIMIT
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = min(old_limit, t_start + max(1.0, float(max_seconds)))
    _TIME_LIMIT = deadline
    try:
        best = a.copy()
        best_sc = float(compute_score(a))
        cur = a.copy()
        cur_sc = best_sc
        theta = np.log(cur + 1e-15)
        theta = theta - float(np.max(theta))

        alphas = [0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005]
        no_improve = 0

        for _ in range(int(max(0, n_iter))):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break

            c = fast_conv(cur)
            L = c.size
            if L == 0:
                break

            if beta_p > 1.0:
                cm = float(np.max(c))
                if not np.isfinite(cm):
                    break
                scaled = float(beta_p) * (c - cm)
                scaled = np.minimum(scaled, 0.0)
                ep = np.exp(scaled)
                total = float(np.sum(ep))
                if total <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(total):
                    p = None
                else:
                    p = ep / total
                    p[p < 1e-12] = 0.0
                    sp = float(np.sum(p))
                    if sp <= 0.0:
                        p = None
            else:
                p = None

            if p is None:
                K = int(min(max(1, top_k), L))
                if K >= L:
                    idx = np.arange(L)
                else:
                    idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
                p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                p[idx] = 1.0 / K

            q = 2.0 * conv_p_rev(p, cur)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
                q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)

            # Gradient of the weighted max M_p with respect to theta in softmax space.
            w = float(np.dot(cur, q))
            g = cur * (q - w * cur)
            mg = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
            if mg < 1e-14:
                break
            g = g / mg

            improved = False
            for alpha in alphas:
                theta_try = theta - float(alpha) * g
                a_try = softmax_from_theta(theta_try)
                sc = float(compute_score(a_try))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best = a_try.copy()
                    cur = a_try.copy()
                    cur_sc = sc
                    theta = theta_try.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break

            if not improved:
                # Very small safeguard step.
                theta_try = theta - 1e-4 * g
                a_try = softmax_from_theta(theta_try)
                sc = float(compute_score(a_try))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc:
                    cur = a_try.copy()
                    cur_sc = sc
                    theta = theta_try.copy()
                    improved = True
                    if sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best = a_try.copy()

            if improved:
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve >= 8:
                    break

        return best_sc, best
    finally:
        _TIME_LIMIT = old_limit


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Parametric continuous-ish starting shapes
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def beta_sequence(n, alpha, beta):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    alpha = float(max(0.0, alpha))
    beta = float(max(0.0, beta))
    # 0**0 is treated as 1, as desired for endpoint-supported beta shapes.
    f = np.power(x, alpha) * np.power(1.0 - x, beta)
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    f = np.clip(f, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(f))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return f / s


def scan_beta(n, quick=True):
    _check_timeout()
    if quick:
        params = [0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0]
    else:
        params = [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0]
    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    best_alpha = 1.0
    best_beta = 1.0
    n = int(n)
    for al in params:
        for be in params:
            _check_timeout()
            a = beta_sequence(n, al, be)
            sc = float(compute_score(a))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a
                best_alpha = float(al)
                best_beta = float(be)
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = beta_sequence(n, 1.0, 1.0)
        best_sc = float(compute_score(best_a))
    return best_a, float(best_sc), float(best_alpha), float(best_beta)


def shape_params_to_density_k3(params, n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64)
    if params.size < 9 or not np.all(np.isfinite(params)):
        return beta_sequence(n, 1.0, 1.0)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    wraw = np.exp(params[0:3] - float(np.max(params[0:3])))
    ws = float(np.sum(wraw))
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.ones(3, dtype=np.float64) / 3.0
    else:
        w = wraw / ws

    al = np.exp(params[3:6])
    be = np.exp(params[6:9])
    al = np.clip(al, 0.0, 8.0)
    be = np.clip(be, 0.0, 8.0)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    xf = np.power(x, al[0]) * np.power(1.0 - x, be[0])
    y1 = np.power(x, al[1]) * np.power(1.0 - x, be[1])
    y2 = np.power(x, al[2]) * np.power(1.0 - x, be[2])
    f = w[0] * xf + w[1] * y1 + w[2] * y2
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    f = np.clip(f, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(f))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return beta_sequence(n, 1.0, 1.0)
    return f / s


def mixture_score_k3(params, n):
    _check_timeout()
    f = shape_params_to_density_k3(params, int(n))
    sc = float(compute_score(f))
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e12
    return sc


def optimize_mixture(n_shape, max_seconds, base_alpha, base_beta):
    global _TIME_LIMIT
    old_limit = _TIME_LIMIT
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    _TIME_LIMIT = min(old_limit, t_start + max(10.0, float(max_seconds)))

    a = float(base_alpha) if np.isfinite(base_alpha) and float(base_alpha) > 1e-3 else 1.0
    b = float(base_beta) if np.isfinite(base_beta) and float(base_beta) > 1e-3 else 1.0

    x0 = np.array(
        [
            0.0, -0.7, -1.4,
            float(np.log(a)), float(np.log(b)),
            float(np.log(b)), float(np.log(a)),
            0.0, 0.0,
        ],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    try:
        res = minimize(
            mixture_score_k3,
            x0,
            args=(int(n_shape),),
            method="Powell",
            options={
                "maxiter": 400,
                "maxfev": 3500,
                "xatol": 1e-7,
                "fatol": 1e-12,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        params = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        params = x0

    f = shape_params_to_density_k3(params, int(n_shape))
    sc = float(compute_score(f))
    _TIME_LIMIT = old_limit
    return f, sc


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main continuation optimizer for a fixed length
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def optimize_sequence(a0, time_budget, target_n):
    global _TIME_LIMIT
    time_budget = float(max(0.0, time_budget))
    target_n = int(max(64, target_n))
    a0 = resample_sequence(a0, target_n)

    old_limit = _TIME_LIMIT
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    _TIME_LIMIT = min(old_limit, t_start + max(1.0, time_budget))

    try:
        a = normalize_array(a0)
        best = a.copy()
        best_sc = float(compute_score(a))
        if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
            best_sc = 1e12

        init_sc = best_sc
        if init_sc > 1.8:
            betas = [10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0, 10000.0]
        else:
            betas = [30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0, 10000.0]

        est_iter = estimate_objective_iter_time(target_n)

        for i, beta in enumerate(betas):
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
            if elapsed > time_budget - 30.0:
                break
            n_left = max(1, len(betas) - i)
            remaining = max(10.0, time_budget - elapsed)
            call_seconds = max(10.0, 0.45 * remaining / float(n_left))
            maxiter = int(max(20, min(3500, call_seconds / max(est_iter, 1e-4))))

            theta = np.log(a + 1e-15)
            theta = theta - float(np.max(theta))
            res = run_lbfgs_softmax(theta, beta, maxiter)
            if res is None:
                break

            a_new = softmax_from_theta(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
            sc_new = float(compute_score(a_new))
            if np.isfinite(sc_new):
                if sc_new < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_new
                    best = a_new.copy()
                a = a_new

            # High-temperature phases: add exact top-lag polish.
            if beta >= 300.0:
                elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
                pol_budget = min(90.0, max(5.0, 0.22 * (time_budget - elapsed)))
                if pol_budget > 10.0:
                    scp, ap = softmax_topk_polish(
                        a,
                        n_iter=90,
                        top_k=64,
                        beta_p=0.0,
                        max_seconds=pol_budget,
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(scp) and scp < best_sc:
                        best_sc = scp
                        best = ap.copy()
                    if np.isfinite(scp):
                        a = np.asarray(ap, dtype=np.float64).copy()

        # Final polishing using global top lags and softer lag weights.
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        rem = max(0.0, time_budget - elapsed)
        if rem > 25.0:
            scp, bp = softmax_topk_polish(
                best,
                n_iter=350,
                top_k=128,
                beta_p=0.0,
                max_seconds=min(140.0, 0.60 * rem),
            )
            if np.isfinite(scp) and scp < best_sc:
                best_sc = scp
                best = bp.copy()
                if rem > 60.0:
                    scp2, bp2 = softmax_topk_polish(
                        best,
                        n_iter=200,
                        top_k=512,
                        beta_p=20.0,
                        max_seconds=min(70.0, 0.35 * rem),
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(scp2) and scp2 < best_sc:
                        best_sc = scp2
                        best = bp2
        return float(best_sc), best
    finally:
        _TIME_LIMIT = old_limit


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helper utilities for the top-level search
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_lengths(parent_len, max_n=24576):
    parent_len = int(max(256, parent_len))
    vals = [
        parent_len,
        int(round(parent_len * 1.5)),
        int(round(parent_len * 2.0)),
        16384,
        max_n,
    ]
    out = []
    for v in vals:
        try:
            v = int(round(v))
        except Exception:
            continue
        if v < 256:
            v = 256
        if v > max_n:
            v = max_n
        if v not in out:
            out.append(v)
    out.sort()
    if not out:
        out = [8192]
    if len(out) > 3:
        out = [out[0], out[len(out) // 2], out[-1]]
    return out


def zero_small_sequence(a):
    a = normalize_array(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(compute_score(a))
    if best_sc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best_sc, best
    med = float(np.median(a))
    if med <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(med):
        med = 1.0 / max(1, a.size)
    for frac in [1e-4, 1e-5, 1e-6]:
        b = a.copy()
        b[b < frac * max(med, 1e-300)] = 0.0
        s = float(np.sum(b))
        if s > 0.0:
            b = b / s
            sc = float(compute_score(b))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = b
    return float(best_sc), best


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Candidate generator
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _TIME_LIMIT
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(max(120.0, float(budget_s)))
    hard_deadline = t_start + max(budget - 30.0, 60.0)
    _TIME_LIMIT = hard_deadline

    # Load parent solution if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        parent = sanitize_sequence(height_sequence_1)
    except Exception:
        parent = None
    if parent is None or parent.size < 64:
        parent = beta_sequence(8192, 1.0, 1.0)
    parent = normalize_array(parent)

    best_a = parent.copy()
    best_score = float(compute_score(parent))
    base_seqs = [parent.copy()]

    print(f"Parent length={parent.size}, parent score={best_score:.8f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Low-resolution global shape search:
    #   1) quick beta scan, 2) mixture-of-betas optimization.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    n_shape = 2048
    time_mix = min(240.0, 0.12 * budget)

    try:
        a_beta_shape, sc_beta_shape, al_beta, be_beta = scan_beta(n_shape, quick=True)
        if a_beta_shape is not None:
            base_seqs.append(a_beta_shape)
            if sc_beta_shape < best_score:
                best_score = sc_beta_shape
                best_a = a_beta_shape.copy()
            print(f"Beta scan (n={n_shape}) score={sc_beta_shape:.8f}")
    except Exception as e:
        al_beta, be_beta = 1.0, 1.0
        print("Beta scan failed:", repr(e))

    try:
        a_mix, sc_mix = optimize_mixture(
            n_shape,
            time_mix,
            al_beta if np.isfinite(al_beta) and al_beta > 0 else 1.0,
            be_beta if np.isfinite(be_beta) and be_beta > 0 else 1.0,
        )
        if a_mix is not None:
            base_seqs.append(a_mix)
            print(f"Mixture shape (n={n_shape}) score={sc_mix:.8f}")
            if sc_mix < best_score:
                best_score = sc_mix
                best_a = a_mix.copy()
    except Exception as e:
        print("Mixture optimization failed:", repr(e))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Short full-length optimization at low resolution to refine shape.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    n_low = 1024
    time_low = min(180.0, 0.08 * budget)
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 120.0:
        low_init = None
        low_init_sc = np.inf
        for base in base_seqs:
            if base is None:
                continue
            a0 = resample_sequence(base, n_low)
            sc0 = float(compute_score(a0))
            if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < low_init_sc:
                low_init_sc = sc0
                low_init = a0
        if low_init is None:
            low_init = resample_sequence(parent, n_low)
            low_init_sc = float(compute_score(low_init))

        try:
            sc_low, a_low = optimize_sequence(low_init, time_low, n_low)
            if np.isfinite(sc_low) and sc_low < best_score:
                best_score = sc_low
                best_a = a_low.copy()
            base_seqs.append(resample_sequence(a_low, len(a_low)))
            print(f"Low-res optimized (n={n_low}) score={sc_low:.8f}, best={best_score:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print("Low-res optimization failed:", repr(e))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Hierarchical full-length optimization.
    # Start near the parent length, then upscale to larger lengths.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    lengths = make_lengths(parent.size, max_n=24576)
    # Avoid re-optimizing very short lengths already covered.
    lengths = [n for n in lengths if n >= 2048]
    if not lengths:
        lengths = [parent.size]

    large_budget = max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0)
    if len(lengths) == 1:
        alloc = [large_budget]
    elif len(lengths) == 2:
        alloc = [0.42 * large_budget, 0.58 * large_budget]
    else:
        alloc = [0.30 * large_budget, 0.35 * large_budget, 0.35 * large_budget]

    for idx, n in enumerate(lengths):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        tb = float(alloc[idx]) if idx < len(alloc) else 0.0
        tb = min(tb, remaining - 30.0)
        if tb < 30.0:
            break

        # Pick the best warm-start for this length.
        init_a = None
        init_sc = np.inf

        for base in base_seqs:
            if base is None:
                continue
            a0 = resample_sequence(base, n)
            sc0 = float(compute_score(a0))
            if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < init_sc:
                init_sc = sc0
                init_a = a0

        # If the current best is already this length, ensure it is included.
        if best_a.size == n:
            sc0 = float(compute_score(best_a))
            if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < init_sc:
                init_sc = sc0
                init_a = best_a.copy()

        # Quick beta alternative for moderate lengths.
        if n <= 16384:
            try:
                a_b, sc_b, _, _ = scan_beta(n, quick=True)
                if np.isfinite(sc_b) and sc_b < init_sc:
                    init_sc = sc_b
                    init_a = a_b
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Smoothed alternative, often useful after upsampling.
        if init_a is not None:
            a_sm = lowpass_sequence(init_a, sigma=0.25)
            sc_sm = float(compute_score(a_sm))
            if np.isfinite(sc_sm) and sc_sm < init_sc:
                init_sc = sc_sm
                init_a = a_sm

        if init_a is None:
            init_a = resample_sequence(best_a, n)
            init_sc = float(compute_score(init_a))

        print(f"\nOptimizing length n={n}, initial score={init_sc:.8f}, time={tb:.0f}s")
        try:
            sc_opt, a_opt = optimize_sequence(init_a, tb, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
                best_score = sc_opt
                best_a = a_opt.copy()
            a_opt = normalize_array(a_opt)
            base_seqs.append(a_opt)
            print(f"Length {n} optimized score={sc_opt:.8f}, global best={best_score:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print("Length optimization failed:", repr(e))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final polish at the current best length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 45.0:
        try:
            sc_pol, a_pol = softmax_topk_polish(
                best_a,
                n_iter=500,
                top_k=192,
                beta_p=0.0,
                max_seconds=min(160.0, 0.55 * remaining),
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_pol) and sc_pol < best_score:
                best_score = sc_pol
                best_a = a_pol.copy()
            print(f"Final top-lag polish score={sc_pol:.8f}, global best={best_score:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print("Final polish failed:", repr(e))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Optional zeroing of very small entries, then finalize.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 10.0:
        try:
            sc_z, a_z = zero_small_sequence(best_a)
            if np.isfinite(sc_z) and sc_z < best_score:
                best_score = sc_z
                best_a = a_z.copy()
            else:
                best_a = normalize_array(a_z)
        except Exception:
            best_a = normalize_array(best_a)

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(best_a), best_a, 0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(best_a))
    if s <= 0.0:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    else:
        best_a = best_a / s

    out = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    # A short verification if it will not be expensive.  The scoring system
    # will evaluate the returned sequence externally anyway.
    try:
        if len(out) <= 12000:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            print(f"Verified evaluate_sequence length={len(out)} value={val:.10f}")
        else:
            print(f"Skipping explicit verification for length={len(out)}; internal best={best_score:.10f}")
    except Exception:
        print(f"Verification skipped; internal best={best_score:.10f}")

    elapsed_total = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print(f"Final length={len(out)}, internal={best_score:.10f}, elapsed={elapsed_total:.1f}s")
    return out
```
